The living river trace feeds barges only. | New work. Queue it after the anchor fix, and re-run the crossings at the corrected dots: Aldrakhar, Iet Ford and Bash Dargis gain box ink, and Stony Ford appears. |

### 5.3 Gates, pins and rulings, each with its real scope

- **UG3 binds mobile units, not the project** (own-atlas c3 **[D]**, corrected by its failing check). Its atlas clause says that a mobile unit's diff touches none of the five pinned functions and that `cityCanvasSha` equals B0 on all five profiles. A registration change that must edit a pinned function lands as a **non-mobile central commit** with a declared rebase.
- **`cityCanvasSha` cannot be satisfied by re-baselining away the procedural raster.** `tools/mobile-capture.js` descends into Tamaron, Sokundo and Kanae and hashes every `cityReg` entry with `prov 'invented'` and a `data:` url. If no such entry exists, the sha is null and `shas.some(x => !x)` fails whatever the baseline (own-atlas c4 **[D]**; the holding check is the corrected one). A vector plan that *replaces* the registered `data:` canvas therefore needs a deliberate central change to the gate. A vector plan *beside* it does not.
- **`cityBox` is not pinned, and that is a blind spot, not a route** (own-atlas b5). `atlas_fn_sha` hashes only the five function sources. A change confined to `cityBox` or to `registerCityOverlay` options leaves it intact, even though `washMake`'s output bounds follow `cityBox`. Nobody should plan around the gap. The procedural plans read `rW` and the anchor inside the pinned functions, so their placement cannot move through `cityBox` alone.
- **Flag-1 literals must keep resolving** (own-atlas b8). The filigree and street scripts and mobile UG4 check `'function registerCityOverlay'`, `'function washMake'`, `'function sampleCityMask'` and `'function genCityCanvas'` (plus `function xmur3`, `var Z_STREET=`, `function worldOpacityUpdate`, `window.ATLAS=` and others) by `indexOf`. The rule is add, never rename.
- **Panes are frozen per track, not by one rule** (own-atlas c6 **[D]**). UG7 binds mobile units, LC13 binds the living layer, and Filigree 2's pre-filigree pane list is a proposal. The pin-safe home for a vector city or a seam fill is the existing panes: `pCity` (450) for geometry, `pCityWash` (449) for the wash and fill, `pCityPOI` (650) for hover marks. No `createPane`, no WebGL. A seam fill must be an `L.imageOverlay` with `{pane:'pCityWash', zIndex:2}`. An `L.polygon` there lands in a per-pane `<svg>` that the re-appended wash `<img>` covers (G8-5).
- **A-U11 is a sequencing rule until Mobile 6 passes** (own-atlas b7 **[D]**, G0-13 **[D]**). Its acceptance requires that "the final shot equals R1 at max zoom; registerCityOverlay literal kept". Until it passes, any change to `art/overlay-Epeshu.jpg`, to its `registerCityOverlay` call or to its placement collides with that acceptance. After it passes, a bounds override read in `ensureCityOverlay` is independent of the preview swap **if** A-U11 swaps with `setUrl`. A second `registerCityOverlay` call would run `overlayCache.delete` and `ensureCityOverlay`, tearing down wash, stubs and POIs, and resetting `prov` if opts are omitted (gap-mobile:C3).
- **R18 does not gate base-atlas registration** (own-atlas c7 **[D]**). Filigree work ships behind the default-off `filigree=1` toggle, which the owner flips after Job 4 by decision, not automatically. A registration record outside the filigree block reaches every reader. It still respects the mobile pins.
- **R4, R7 and the tap resolver govern interactive glyphs, not the whole city** (own-atlas d1 **[D]**, d2 **[D]**, d3). Under the R7 override, nothing is drawn at rest on the city sheet (z ≥ Z_TIER_D 7.5). One glyph centre within 22 CSS px opens its card, two or more open a chooser, and a 650 ms long-press shows the name. R4 and B-34 forbid labels on blocks, houses or lanes at rest. FM4 makes every filigree feature register with the single coarse-pointer tap resolver that A-U3 is to build. **That resolver does not exist at HEAD** (A-U2 failed, A-U3 and A-U4 skipped). Interactive city work therefore waits for Mobile 5 to land.
- **Mobile A-U7, A-U8 and A-U9 change zoom-step and panel geometry on touch** (gap-mobile:C6, C7 **[D]**, C8 **[D]**). Once Mobile 8 lands, one coarse-pointer button press is 0.5 zoom (the overlay ramp takes 2 presses instead of 4). Panel fly-target offsets should be computed from the sheet's settled geometry, not a constant or a device class. These are spec-derived figures.
- **The density bible's sha is the Filigree 2 preflight** (own-atlas v1). `todo-inputs.json` reaches Filigree 2 only through the bible. Any `density-bible.json` edit (a prerequisites row, a rewritten number) forces a Path B re-integration of Filigree 1 before Filigree 2.

### 5.4 How a registration item reaches the filigree spec

This matters because, as v5 shows, the spec will otherwise be silent on registration, and slice C will paint through the unregistered `cityBox`.

- **The working route: a README §7 row** (own-atlas v2). Filigree 2 reads `docs/filigree/README.md § "7. Reuse map"` live at run time, in the section writers (`writePrompt`), the integrator (`integPrompt`) and the unit planner (`unitPrompt`). A §7 row about registration or placement that lands **before Filigree 2 runs** reaches the spec without re-running Filigree 1.
- **What does not work** (all refuted; see the closing table):
  - A POLISH-only item never enters the sheet spec. `prereq_unqueued` and `polish_inserts` only dedup the bible's own prerequisites, and Job 3 (R11) gates only on "Traced road network", "Census second pass" and `rivers.json` (own-atlas v4, gap-consumers:C1).
  - A POLISH line titled `- [ ] **Filigree data —` is not free. The street workflows' HOLD_TASK (ST15) holds Street 3 while any such line is open.
  - New ruling ids in `rulings.json` are silently ignored, because `rulingsMerge` walks only the known R1-R22. `rulings.json` already overrides R2, R7, R12, R14 and R18 (own-atlas v3).
- **R21 and B-32 as carriers** (own-atlas b9 **[D]**). Data contradictions are "recorded and queued directly above the job they block", and named layers are built only from data that matches the print. This is a valid **process** route for a registration item, with the side effects above. It is not a fix.
- **Slice letters** (own-atlas v6). The bible's prerequisites table uses F3-C = fiction stack and F3-D = city grain. `filigree-3-build.js`, Filigree 2, the README and POLISH use **C = city, D = stack**. The build convention is the one that runs. Cite by letter and slice word.

### 5.5 The sim side: what it owns and where it disagrees

- **The sim is registered to the atlas at realm scale only** (own-sim S9, S10 **[D]**). `index.html` never reads the DM chart: there are zero hits for `overlay-Epeshu` or `charts/epeshu`. The Epēshu heightfield and art are traced from the atlas window [1060,1240]..[1860,2040]. Epēshu's grid cell (169,168) maps to atlas (1236.04,1415.00) by the cell-corner convention, 0.06 px from the anchor. The cell-centre convention documented in `tools/filigree-dem.js` gives about (1236.56,1415.52), which is what the failing check disputed.
- **The interior disagrees with the DM chart** (own-sim S8). Projected through `worldPOICoord`'s `cityBox`, the sim's 13 Epēshu labels and landmarks miss the chart POIs by 103-488 m (median about 190 m, 9-43 atlas px). Examples: Old Quarry Lot 488 m, Eelwynd 394 m, Thūkīn 378 m, Outwall 299 m. This is a secondary, sim-side **[reg]** gap, not a gate on the atlas work. Its reference `cityBox` is itself unregistered.
- **Size is a constant on both sides** (own-sim S11). The sim's capital disc is `rad = 265` m. The atlas box is FOOT_R 26, about ±24.2 × ±26 atlas px. Neither comes from the chart, so canon registration must come from tie points.
- **Names drift** (own-sim S7). The canon spellings are the chart's: `chart-pois.json` is transcribed from the DM chart ("Marble Quarter", "Thūkīn Amphitheatre", "The Fairy Arch"). The sim says "the Marble Quarter", "the Thūkīn Amphitheater" and "Fairy Arch". This matters only to a future name-keyed hand-off; the atlas `#simLink` carries no `goto=` today.
- **`#goto=` cannot reach districts** (own-sim S6). `applyGotoFromHash` matches only `W.settlements` and `CUSTOM_MAP_DATA.extraLandmarks` names and otherwise ignores the value. "Epēshīn Forum", LC7's default first exhibit, is a `cityLabels` entry, so the planned H2 step lands on the default view. Nothing owns this gap: ST1 keeps street inside its block, and continuity is vocabulary only. Unrelated to registration and medium.
- **The H2z poster is a perspective view** (own-sim S4 **[D]**). It will be shot from the Street camera pose in `docs/street/gates/views.json` (SV1: "Epēshīn Forum", R=30, low pitch, yaw 0), so the overlay-to-poster seam matches by place name only. It is planned, not built, and it is **not** the source of today's shift, which is registration in the z6.8-7.8 band.
- **Re-registering `cityLabels` is the cheap half** (own-sim S13 **[D]**, S14). The labels are copied with `if(d.cityLabels) s.cityLabels = d.cityLabels;` in a block "applied AFTER the draw so the RNG stream is untouched". By code reading (not measured), moving them leaves the `simDays(400)` fingerprint unchanged. The cost is fixtures: SV1, SV3, SV6 and SV8 resolve their focus from these labels ("Epēshīn Forum", "Whitestreet", "the Marble Quarter", "Wood Quay"), and the Living posters reuse those poses. **Cheapest before Street 1 freezes `views.json`.** It must be its own central item, because street owns only its block and living never writes the sim.
- **Re-planning the sim's streets is an LC17-class change** (own-sim S12, S25, G7-13 **[D]**). `genTownPlans` runs `planStreetsFor(s, R)` for every settlement on `W.rng.gen`, and `GEN_STEPS` then runs `genBuildings`, `genHouses` and `genFlora` on the same stream, with draws per street segment. Any change in Epēshu's segment count therefore re-baselines the whole `#s=epeshu` world: buildings, great houses, flora and the chronicle that follows. A sibling stream removes the `planStreetsFor` draws but not the `genBuildings` ones. Owner-gated, never a default, after Street 4 and the street hold, with the re-baseline recorded per consumer. **Keep the sim out of the first cut.**
- **One trace, two consumers, but not one fabric** (G7-12 **[D]**). One chart-conditioned trace can feed both apps through one transform: art px → atlas (per-city record) → sim at 11.2647 m per atlas px. The seeded fabric cannot share one scale. At the candidate placements one art px is 0.11-0.19 m, so the prototype's 12×18 art-px lots come out 1.4-2.3 × 2.1-3.4 m, against the sim's `planParcelsFor` lots of 8.5-11 × 7.5-10.5 m. The holding check reframed this: the atlas fabric is a drawn symbolisation with no metric lot size, so the choice is not "sub-metre sim houses or about 270 atlas houses". It belongs with the registration scale question.
- **Street has already flagged the layout risk** (own-sim S15 **[D]**). `docs/street/todo-inputs.json` lists "Epēshu's districts are labels only; authoring a layout may conflict with canon or the atlas chart", and Street 1's q11 reads `chart-pois.json` against `cityLabels`. That is where S8 will surface. It is a coordination constraint, not a fix.
- **A candidate tie point** (own-sim S16 **[D]**). ST12 rules the Blue Temple of Thobrauk on Wood Quay's northern edge as the one landmark facade, and the chart agrees in direction: Temple of Thobrauk Tegendes (1053,1341) is 446 chart px north of Wood Quay (1009,1787). The failing check disputed "both apps already honour it". Only the atlas chart places it today, so it is a *candidate* first control point, not a shared one.
- **Gates and entries** (own-sim S22 **[D]**). A vector city should take its road-to-gate tie points from the atlas's own traced approaches (`cityApproaches` → `mkGate`). Agreement with the sim's `s.entries` (from `registerRoadEntries`) has never been measured. Only the atlas side applies now.

### 5.6 Hash and delivery accounting for registration moves

| move | what it changes | what it leaves alone | source |
|---|---|---|---|
| Canon per-city record (bounds or affine for Epēshu, Summarch) | Atlas shots at city zooms, LV5/LV6 stills (none frozen yet), the B-38 and q08-c08 numbers, which go stale and are not a registration baseline (never tune a fit against them; refuted gap-consumers:C2) | `atlas_fn_sha`, `cityCanvasSha` (canon is not hashed), TILES_V (no raster bytes change) | gap-consumers:C5 **[D]**, C7; refuted gap-mobile:C10 |
| Anchor moves for Tamaron, Sokundo, Kanae | `cityCanvasSha` on every profile, through `sampleCityMask`'s window `Math.round((atlasX-1.35*rW)*2)`. Needs an owner-approved B0/R1 re-cut. | Marker-keyed mobile control ids and tap fixtures | gap-consumers:C5 **[D]**, refuted C6 |
| Point convention (ring centre vs marker vs anchor) | A different mask window and expected hash for each choice. Tamaron's sea set is [319.3,1.6] at the ring centre and [317.7,113.6,3.5] at ring-(1,1). **Rule the convention and re-measure or freeze the sea fields before any B0 cut**, or the cut has to be redone. The hash differences are inferred, not recomputed. | none | G0-3 |
| Anchors with no same-named marker (17, incl. Sokundo, Kanae, Lepon the Old) | `maps-site/data/gazetteer.json`; regenerate with `tools/build-gazetteer.js`, never hand-edit | the 119 marker-shadowed anchors | gap-consumers:C11 **[D]** |
| Any anchor derivation | The living sea-mask done-when pass set (14 → 21 of 38 under an approximated rule; one breaks). Its rebaseline scope must include that check and re-measured `sea.side`/`sea.distPx`. | | gap-anchor:C5 **[D]** |
| Plan rivers fed into the RV block | `atlas_fn_sha.genCityCanvas` (rebase the references) | `cityCanvasSha`, if engagement is RV-guarded (none of the hashed trio is a river city) | G0-9 |
| Mask-path fix (halo-as-sea, rivers into `sampleCityMask`, a new land/water source for Penthelon) | `atlas_fn_sha` **and** `cityCanvasSha` (all three hashed cities are harbours) | | G0-9, gap-anchor:C3 |
| Seam clip as CSS `clip-path` polygon or inline SVG `data:` mask on the overlay `<img>` | Shots only | Pins, TILES_V, A-U11's file | gap-consumers:C5 **[D]**, G8-8 **[D]** |
| Seam clip inside `genCityCanvas` / `sampleCityMask` / `washMake`, or on a new pane | Pins (and UG7 for a pane) | | gap-consumers:C5 **[D]** |
| Pre-warped or feathered plate, PNG mask under `art/` | TILES_V (one global over 3,364 rasters, 36.7 MB, so every returning device refetches), manifest after D7, A-U11's same-file acceptance until Mobile 6 passes | | G0-13 **[D]**, G8-8 **[D]** |

The anchor work itself has grown (G0-1). With G1's audit of the 98 anchors the table never covered, the verdicts are **45 move, 2 keep, 4 owner calls** over 51 audited anchors of 138. The other 87 are on their printed dots. The eight additions are Sokundo, Kanae, Shengo, Pomos, Ishin, Pish, Ītor Hāspira and Paril. Sokundo and Kanae were already in the rebaseline scope, so the rebaseline scope does not widen. Ring centres from the HEAD z5 tiles: Sokundo ○ (1478.0,1450.0), 5.81 px off; Kanae ○ (1523.0,1490.0), 7.45 px off (G0-2 **[D]**; the failing check accepted the figures but disputed treating them as settled targets ahead of the convention ruling).

The rule behind the verdicts is that the printed glyph on PatrinorModern is the truth, and neither record is trusted alone (gap-anchor:C1 **[D]**, C2 **[D]**). Derive anchors in an offline deterministic tool with explicit overrides, not a runtime marker-wins rule: Drāmūz's marker is the record in error. Moving an anchor fixes only the centre. The scale and orientation of DM art inside `cityBox` remain unregistered. The 'keep' verdict covers two different cases. For Drāmūz, the anchor is right and the marker is to be fixed. For Sjefenhold, an uncharted canon place is kept with an explicit offDot reason.

#### Supporting facts that shape ownership (one line each; detail in sections 1-4)

- **[reg]** The canon charts are registered only through `cityBox`. `registerCityOverlay` stores `{url,w,h,prov,provText,poiRef,pois,stubs}` and no control points, and the POIs are reprojected linearly into the same box (own-atlas b1). Misregistration is largest there. Seeded plans are *not* registered by construction in general: the z5 mask shapes a plan only where the patch holds real sea (`drawWater` and `mask.water`) (own-atlas b2 **[D]**; the failing check disputed "already fit the atlas coast").
- **[reg]** `overlay-Epeshu.jpg` is portrait 2863×3072 (the brief had it swapped), the same aspect as the 3914×4200 chart that `chart-pois.json` is indexed in. One transform therefore moves overlay, POIs and any chart-derived vector together (own-sim S28).
- **[reg]** The print mask is the right registration target and clip reference. Near Epēshu the DEM 0 m contour agrees with it to about 1.3-1.5 atlas px rms (gap-coasts tc-1 **[D]**; the failing check disputed the window-wide "one coast" framing as one-directional). B-38's "DEM shore up to 10 atlas px off" measured the DEM against the chart as `cityBox` places it. The chart is the outlier, so filigree contours should clip to the print mask (gap-coasts tc-6).
- **[reg]** Era sheets need no per-era registration fields. `setEra` swaps only the base layer on the same bounds, and the coasts match. What remains is the wash and plan land hue (about ΔG 29, ΔB 21 in Leponnia), which is a filigree look issue (G9-8 **[D]**; the failing check disputed parts of the colour wording).
- **[reg vs medium]** Vectors alone do not anchor. The Road that Walks with the Sun is already an `L.polyline` (`buildNamedWays`) and sits a median 31.3 atlas px off the printed road ink (p90 64.6, max 98.2) (gap-print vop-01 **[D]**; the holding check corrected that this is static misregistration, not the zoom shift). The references agree. swisstopo's 2D→3D hand-off transfers view state, not a picture (gap-refs RA-swisstopo-3d). Its identify is a coordinate lookup (10 px tolerance, 10-feature cap), not a geometry hit-test (RA-swisstopo-buildings-overlays **[D]**). Leaflet's CRS.Simple guide and Allmaps fit placement from measured control points (RA-tools **[D]**: port the method, offline and stored as data, for the two canon overlays only). Collison's Bay Atlas and Azlen's flyover are owner's-brief references for the look and the single-frame principle, unverified as web facts (RA-collison-bayatlas **[D]**, RA-azlen **[D]**). The Lombard Street and How to Train Your Dragon references have no verifiable source and are not anchoring precedents (RA-lombard-httyd **[D]**).
- **[medium]** Epēshu's live atlas ceiling is `cityMaxZ` = min(11.5, 4+log2(3072/52)) = 9.885 (×59.08 on screen). Z_STREET 11.5 is the global ceiling; no street hand-off exists in code (gap-coasts tc-8 **[D]**). At 9.885 the raster is one texel per CSS px, so it goes soft from z8.885 at DPR 2 and z8.3 at DPR 3, and `flyIntoCity` lands at 0.471 texels per device px on an iPhone 13 (G0-18 **[D]**). A higher-res source registered at its own dims raises the cap by exactly its gain, so only decoupling the cap changes deepest-zoom crispness (G0-17).
- **[medium]** One generated plan is live at a time, about 500-2,100 features. SVG works only merged by class into about 11-30 paths. The claim that R4/R7 license the merge overreached (gap-vecbudget:C3 **[D]**). A town building is 1-2 screen px in the fade band, so a vector city needs level of detail or a raster hand-off below about z8 (gap-vecbudget:C5). `genCityCanvas` is deterministic and clock-free under a stub, but it paints its own canvas and returns no geometry, so a vector consumer needs a sibling function (gap-vecbudget:C8 **[D]**).
- **[medium]** What can be traced from the canon charts is coast/water, Summarch's wall ring and Epēshu's hinterland edge. Main streets are unpainted ground, and Epēshu's wall cannot be separated. Epēshu has 23,184 building blobs at ≥12 px, not 17,323 (G0-20). A pre-warped plate bends the DM fabric (Wood Quay moves 3.5 atlas px at 0.6x+3E, 3.6% of land folds at 1.0x) for only a 36-38% cut in misregistered area. That counts against a coast-band warp. The wall-strain figures are held until the wall question is settled (G0-14).
- **[process]** The seam clip shape should be an offline percent polygon registered to the atlas coast. A runtime canvas mask from the z5 tiles is rejected on crispness alone: one mask px is about 30 screen px at 9.885 (G8-7 **[D]**; the determinism and duplication arguments were disputed). A PNG mask under `art/` costs a TILES_V bump and is the fallback only (G8-8 **[D]**).
- **[process]** Tier figures should be quoted as HEAD pre-A-U2 values: iPhone 13 fit 1.9, tier C 3.9667; pixel7 fit 2.4; spread from desktop C 4.7833 is 0.82, and 1.22 with landscape. They cannot be skipped, because A-U2 failed (gap-mobile:C2).
- LC4 (creatures) stays a living owner question that this ask does not touch (own-sim S26 **[D]**; the failing check noted only that "untouched" is the analyst's inference).

### 5.7 Sequencing these constraints imply (recommendation, not a decision)

1. **Now, docs only (no app file).** Write the per-city registration policy, the anchor verdict table (45/2/4) and the point-convention question into a README §7 reuse-map row **before Filigree 2 runs**, so the sheet spec stops being silent (own-atlas v2, v5). This needs no Filigree 1 re-run. Caveat: v2's holding check names three limits on this route, and only the first (the live read at run time) is visible in the check text, so the other two are open. Avoid the `Filigree data —` title unless holding Street 3 is wanted. Avoid bible edits, which force re-integration (own-atlas v1).
2. **Window A: after Mobile 9 and Mobile 14 are checked, before Filigree 3 starts.** This is the "Atlas reduced motion" precedent: no mobile-build or mobile-review live, never beside Filigree 3, Street 3, Street 4 or a street punch run (own-atlas q1, q2). It falls before Street 3, which is held until Filigree 3 is checked, and before Living 1, which is blocked by Filigree 4. In one central atlas commit:
   - the canon per-city record, read by bounds, POIs, wash, cap and fly target (gap-consumers:C9);
   - the seam clip as an element-level CSS clip plus a `pCityWash` zIndex-2 fill (G8-5);
   - the anchor moves after the convention ruling;
   - the Summarch literal and its data twins;
   - an owner-approved `cityCanvasSha` B0 re-cut;
   - gazetteer regeneration.

   Landing between Mobile 9 and Filigree 2 costs a rebase, not a block, but nothing re-checks it automatically: `filigree-2-plan.js` never reads `state.json` (own-atlas q3). If A-U11 has not passed by then, keep the `registerCityOverlay` literal and the plate bytes unchanged (own-atlas b7 **[D]**).
3. **Window B, if A is missed:** after "Filigree 4 · Review → punch list" is checked, never beside a live Street 3/4 run (own-atlas q2). Registration then lands after the LV5/LV6 freeze and costs one documented re-freeze (own-atlas b12, gap-consumers:C7).
4. **Not inside Filigree 3.** It holds both app files and builds only `sheet-spec.json` units, which do not exist yet. Registration or vector-city work cannot be added to it as extra units (own-atlas q4).
5. **Interactive city features** wait for the A-U3 tap resolver (Mobile 5) and register with it. There is no second tap path (own-atlas d3).
6. **Sim `cityLabels` re-registration**, if the owner wants it: its own central item, **before Street 1** (own-sim S13 **[D]**, S14). Sim street re-planning is an LC17-class owner-gated item after Street 4 and the street hold (own-sim S12, S25).
7. **The vector city comes last**, as its own item (G7-11 **[D]**). It is atlas-only, sits beside the raster, and runs on a sibling stream (for example `xmur3(name+'|vec')`) calling the pinned `mulberry32` (G7-10 **[D]**). It is placed only through the registration record, because through `cityBox` the prototype trace lands exactly where the raster does: 7.7 atlas px rms coast error, 1,780 of 5,424 buildings on print sea (G7-9 **[D]**). It lives in `pCity`, uses 2D Leaflet renderers and hands off to the raster below about z8 (gap-vecbudget:C5). Filigree supplies the look.

### 5.8 Owner questions this section raises or corrects

Q-numbers are those of the prior dossier's (f) list, restated where the checks corrected them. In the deduplicated "Owner questions" below they appear as Q8 → OQ1, Q9 → OQ5 and OQ6, Q12 → OQ14, Q4 → OQ8, Q11 → OQ20, Q16 → OQ21, Q6 → OQ7; the three "New" questions are OQ22, OQ23 and OQ13.

- **Q8 (anchor verdicts), corrected:** approve **45 move / 2 keep / 4 owner calls** (G0-1), not 37/1/2. The four owner calls are Paga, Penthelon, Ūmak-nūs-Ion and Kolens. Confirm the two meanings of "keep": Drāmūz (anchor right, marker fixed as an R21 data item) and Sjefenhold (an uncharted place kept off-dot with a stated reason). Treat Hordon and Clickerhall as anchor errors, not exemptions.
- **Q9 (B0 re-cut), extended:** first rule the point convention (ring centre, marker, or anchor at ring-(1,1)) and whether the trio's `sea` fields are re-measured or frozen. Then approve one `cityCanvasSha` re-cut for Tamaron, Sokundo and Kanae, landing at the Mobile 9 boundary (G0-3).
- **Q12 (who owns registration, how it is queued), refined:** the evidence supports a **standalone atlas registration item** that owns the per-city record, the anchor data and the seam-clip data (G8-10 **[D]**, G7-11 **[D]**). It would reach Filigree 2 through a README §7 row, not through POLISH alone or a bible edit (own-atlas v2; refuted v4, gap-consumers:C1). The skeleton trace of the canon charts (coast, water, Summarch's wall ring) would sit with it, and a full-building trace would be its own owner-gated item. That split is G4's inference, offered here as a proposal (G0-21 **[D]**). Is holding Street 3 (a `Filigree data —` title) wanted?
- **Q4 (Epēshu scale), extended:** two criteria disagree (G0-15 **[D]**). The print coast favours 0.6x+3E (3.58 rms), but at the bottom of the policy range and against the edge of the free fit's search box. The sim's label spread and extent favour about 1.0x or more. The atlas wins for placement and the sim follows. The prototype's trace coast and road exits cannot choose the scale (G7-5 **[D]**, G7-6 **[D]**).
- **Q11 (LC7), reframed:** LC7 asks only about 3D detail and WebGL in `maps-site`. A 2D in-atlas detailed city is a **separate** owner question, not a re-opening of LC7 (own-sim S24 **[D]**). Should the in-atlas city be built at all, and in what relation to the H2z crossfade source, which is currently "the city overlay" (own-sim S3 **[D]**)?
- **Q16 (sim follow), narrowed:** move the sim's Epēshu `cityLabels` toward the registered chart POIs before Street 1 (fixtures, not fingerprint)? Leave `planStreetsFor` alone until an LC17-class ruling? Do not drag the sim along with atlas anchor moves: cross-app links are name-keyed and `maps-site` reads no sim grid (refuted gap-consumers:C8).
- **Q6 (Summarch), extended:** the literal (523,58) also lives in `factions.json` (the Allhammers seat, read by "show on the map"), `party.json` and `party-route.json`. Do they move with it (refuted gap-consumers:C10)?
- **New: canon district spellings.** Should the sim adopt the chart's spellings and articles ("Marble Quarter", "Thūkīn Amphitheatre", "The Fairy Arch"), and should "Sim ↔ atlas continuity" add district (`cityLabels`) targets to `#goto=`, so that LC7's "Epēshīn Forum" step lands (own-sim S6, S7)?
- **New: the Blue Temple / Wood Quay pair as the first Epēshu control point** (own-sim S16 **[D]**)? It needs the sim to place the temple first if it is to be shared.
- **New: `setMaxZoom` ease-down.** Nobody owns this unrequested camera move (own-atlas a3 **[D]**). Should it ride with the registration item's lifecycle fixes?

## 6. Owner questions

Deduplicated from the five sections. These are choices for the owner; nothing here is decided. The bracketed prior number is the question's number in the previous draft's (f) list, which sections 3 and 5 still cite. Given throughout, as the brief's stated constraint: the atlas geography is authoritative, so registration bends art to the atlas and never the atlas to art. `findings.json` `owner_questions[].raw` names the section entries each question merges.

- **OQ1 (prior Q8) · Anchors.** Anchor verdict table. The audit now reads 45 move / 2 keep / 4 owner calls over the 51 audited anchors of 138 (it replaces the earlier 37/1/2; the other 87 already sit on a printed ring). The two keeps mean different things: Drāmūz (its anchor is on the printed ◉; its marker, 35 px S in open water, is the record to fix) and Sjefenhold (an uncharted canon place kept off-dot with an explicit reason). Under this table Hordon and Clickerhall are anchor errors, not exemptions. The table would be derived by an offline deterministic tool against a reviewed by-name exception table, with no runtime marker-wins rule and no distance-only snap. Choose: (a) approve as stated; (b) approve with changes to named rows; (c) hold it until the census second pass has landed.
- **OQ2 (prior Q7, Penthelon part) · Anchors.** Penthelon. Its marker (1942,755) sits on the printed ◉, but moving the anchor there drowns the plan: the r−b≤54 colour test reads the olive region tint as water (about 2% land at the ring against about 61% at the current anchor 113 px N), and the 0.9.7 off-dot reason is stale. Choose: (a) move it together with a non-colour land/water source (inked coast or the filigree DEM), which touches the UG3-pinned sampleCityMask path and so atlas_fn_sha and cityCanvasSha; or (b) keep it off-dot with a fresh, stated reason.
- **OQ3 (prior Q7, Paga part) · Anchors.** Paga and Drāmūz. Paga: both its anchor and its marker are off the print, and the printed ○ is near (2074,809); re-snap both records there, or name another target? Drāmūz: fix the marker (35 px S in open water) and keep the anchor, or treat it differently?
- **OQ4 · Anchors.** The remaining owner calls and keeps. Kolens (uncharted; no printed river or ring under it) and Ūmak-nūs-Ion: move, keep, or rule each separately? Sjefenhold: keep off-dot with an explicit reason, or move?
- **OQ5 (prior Q9, first part) · Anchors.** Point convention, to be ruled before any cityCanvasSha B0 re-cut (G0-3 makes it a precondition). Choose: (a) ring centre (pixel-centre detector); (b) census-marker convention, ring −(1,0); (c) today's anchor convention, ring −(1,1). Each gives a different sampleCityMask window, plan, hash and gate set; Tamaron's sea approaches are [319.3,1.6] at the ring centre against [317.7,113.6,3.5] at ring −(1,1). In the same ruling: are the trio's sea fields re-measured in the same edit, or explicitly frozen? The ruling also settles Summarch's ring point, (542.0,50.0) by the detector against (541.75,49.75) by the earlier tie fit.
- **OQ6 (prior Q9) · Anchors.** cityCanvasSha B0 rebaseline for the hashed trio, Tamaron ◉ (1659,1554), Sokundo ○ (1478.0,1450.0) and Kanae ○ (1523.0,1490.0) (anchors, sea.side and road bearings), after OQ5 and a crop check of the Sokundo and Kanae rings. Choose: (a) approve one re-cut landing at the Mobile 9 lane boundary; (b) approve it as a declared rebase at another point; (c) do not move the trio yet.
- **OQ7 (prior Q6) · Canon charts.** Summarch. Move the literal (523,58) to the printed ring as a translation only, keeping FOOT_R 14 and north-up? The target is (541.75,49.75) by the tie fit or (542.0,50.0) by the ring detector, depending on OQ5. Which records move with it: the meshedWorldInit literal, the factions.json Allhammers seat, party.json, and the party-route.json (523,58) waypoint? The second visit at (545,80) is offset on purpose. The DM's river runs north of the ring while the atlas river runs on its other side (canon has the Stony River girdling the southern flank). Choose: redraw the river, move the ring, or accept the mismatch.
- **OQ8 (prior Q4) · Canon charts.** Epēshu placement policy. Given: the atlas decides placement and the sim follows later as a separate item. The evidence: the print coast favours 0.6x of today's box with the centre +3 px E (3.58 atlas px rms), but that is the bottom of the policy range and a free fit runs to the edge of its search box, so coast shape cannot decide it (the holding check on G0-15 suggests picking within about 0.6-0.8x); the sim's hand-placed label spread favours about 1.0x (rms-radius ratio 1.12, or 0.94 without Fairy Arch) and its built-up extent favours 1.0x or more (1.53-1.63x). Choose: (a) the scale, anywhere in roughly 0.6-1.0x (centre shifts +3 to +8 px E were tested); (b) which chart point sits on the printed ring: the Forum, the harbour mouth, or the built-up centroid; (c) whether the atlas fabric is a drawn symbolisation with no metric scale (the chart's drawn density cannot match the sim's metric lots; they differ about 4-7x linear).
- **OQ9 (prior Q5) · Seam.** Epēshu seam band 2: chart sea over print land, about 75 px² at 0.6x+3E. Band 1 (chart land over print sea) is handled by the print-land clip plus a sea fill in pCityWash. Choose for band 2: (a) make it transparent; (b) key the chart's flat sea fill out so the atlas land shows; (c) fill it with land tone; (d) keep the base opaque under the footprint (G8-6 [D] found this is one global opacity, with only about 6% showing through the wash).
- **OQ10 · Canon charts.** Canon art bending. Choose the limit for the DM's Epēshu and Summarch charts: (a) scale plus translate only (new axis-aligned bounds, no resampling); (b) also rotation by Helmert, which keeps shapes but needs a PNG or WebP pre-warp with alpha or a custom overlay subclass; (c) also warps (TPS, polynomial2/3). Only translate plus uniform scale was tested, and one coast-band warp was measured and rejected (G0-11).
- **OQ11 (prior Q15) · Canon charts.** Modal chart. Should enterChart over charts/epeshu and charts/summarch read the same stored chart-px-to-atlas-px transform (for example so Back returns to the registered spot), or stay in its own chart-px frame, which leaves two POI transforms?
- **OQ12 · Audit.** Registration audit. Should the swipe or opacity override (base and overlay forced to opacity 1, at about z7-8) ship behind a URL debug flag in maps-site, or stay tools-only? R7 forbids showing it at rest.
- **OQ13 · Lifecycle.** Lifecycle policy. May worldCityUpdate stop lowering maxZoom below the current zoom (no automatic ease-down), with catchment hysteresis? This changes desktop feel, and the living chart's H2z zoom-through builds on it. Nobody owns this unrequested camera move today. Choose: (a) ride with the registration item's lifecycle fixes; (b) its own item; (c) leave it as the intended soft ceiling.
- **OQ14 (prior Q12) · Ownership.** Ownership and queueing of the registration work. None of these is owned by the filigree, living, street or mobile tracks today: the per-city record, the anchor data, the print-coast ring and sea fill (seam-clip data), the plan-river consumer, and road re-traces with a per-leg medium field in party-route.json. Choose: (a) a standalone atlas registration item (a new POLISH item beside filigree slice C, no density-bible edit) that reaches Filigree 2 through a README §7 row; (b) an amendment to slice C; (c) another home. Also: should the canon skeleton trace (coast/water, Summarch's wall ring, Epēshu's hinterland edge) sit with it, with a full-building or chart-conditioned fabric as its own later item gated on OQ17 and OQ18? Is holding Street 3 wanted (a POLISH title starting 'Filigree data —' does that)?
- **OQ15 (prior Q17) · Roads.** Road data. Re-trace the Road that Walks with the Sun and the sea-lane vertex [1320,1398] onto the printed ink now (data-only, but it changes generated gates and so cityCanvasSha), or wait for POLISH 'Traced road network'?
- **OQ16 (prior Q10) · Scale.** Mile constant. Choose: the printed bar (659 atlas px = 600 mi, 0.910 mi/px) or MI_PER_NATIVE=600/723 (0.830 mi/px, origin undocumented).
- **OQ17 (prior Q18) · Canon charts.** DM masters. Both canon charts look like Watabou MFCG output. Does the DM hold higher-resolution or vector masters, such as MFCG seeds or permalinks or GeoJSON exports, for Epēshu and Summarch? The proposal is to ask before any tracing; tracing finds no street network in the charts, because the lanes are unpainted ground.
- **OQ18 (prior Q1) · Vector city.** What the vector idea is for. The DM raster is 1 texel per CSS px at Epēshu's cityMaxZ 9.885, so on phones it goes soft from z8.3 (DPR 3) and z8.885 (DPR 2), and flyIntoCity lands at 0.471 texels per device px on an iPhone 13. Choose: (a) crispness only, which a higher-resolution registered raster also delivers; or a drawn, interactive city as (b) a vector layer replacing the chart, (c) vector under the chart as hit geometry only, or (d) vector only past the chart's native zoom.
- **OQ19 (prior Q3) · Vector city.** Hit-testing. The city has nothing at rest (R7), no block labels (R4) and a 22 CSS px tap resolver (FM4). Should it carry building or block hit-testing at all? If yes, choose: a coordinate lookup through the registration transform, or vector paths. The prototype's drawTrace draws POI discs at rest, which R7 forbids.
- **OQ20 (prior Q11) · Vector city.** LC7 and a 2D in-atlas city. Confirm or override LC7 (the sim is the only 3D; no WebGL in maps-site; H2z crossfades into a Street poster). If confirmed, any vector city is a 2D Leaflet layer and the prototype's three.js mode stays out of the atlas. Separately, since LC7 asks only about 3D/WebGL: should a 2D in-atlas detailed city be built at all, and should it, or the raster overlay (today 'the city overlay'), be the H2z crossfade source?
- **OQ21 (prior Q16) · Sim.** How far the sim follows. Choose any of: (a) move the sim's Epēshu cityLabels toward the registered chart POIs before Street 1 (costs frozen-view fixtures SV1/SV3/SV6/SV8, not the fingerprint, by code reading); (b) leave planStreetsFor alone until an LC17-class ruling; (c) treat sim adoption of chart geometry for Epēshu, which re-baselines the whole #s=epeshu world through W.rng.gen (buildings, great houses, flora, chronicle), as a separate owner-gated item after the street hold, or never. Keep the sim out of the first cut?
- **OQ22 · Sim.** Canon district names and deep links. Should the sim adopt the chart's canon spellings ('Marble Quarter', 'Thūkīn Amphitheatre', 'The Fairy Arch')? Should 'Sim ↔ atlas continuity' add cityLabels targets to #goto= so that LC7's 'Epēshīn Forum' step lands?
- **OQ23 · Sim.** First Epēshu control point. Use the Blue Temple of Thobrauk / Wood Quay pair? To be shared, the sim would have to place the temple first.
- **OQ24 · Living.** View-state hash keys. May the living track's H2/H2z link add atlas hash keys for view state (an atlas-px centre plus a scale)? Today setHash writes only #place= and #chart=, and the sim's applyGotoFromHash reads only a name.

## 7. Browser probes still needed

Deduplicated from the five sections, with exact steps. The next phase runs these on a HEAD copy with its own server: never port 8544, never `tools/mobile-capture.js`, never beside a mobile capture. Ids keep the sections' labels (P-numbers, R1-R3, and S13/S22/G0-3 after the item each probe settles); `findings.json` `measure_probes[].raw` names the section entries each one merges. P3 does not exist in the section lists.

- **P0.** Common setup for every probe. Run in a later session, after Mobile 5 lands, on a HEAD copy (an exact export of the committed repo) served by the probing session's own static server on a free port other than 8544, written below as http://localhost:<port>/. Never use port 8544, never run tools/mobile-capture.js, and never run beside a mobile capture (it holds a global lock). Throwaway pages and edited copies go in a mktemp -d directory served by that same server. Profiles: desktop 1366x768@1, desktop2x 1440x900@2, iphone13 390x664@3 touch, and pixel7 where noted (4x CPU throttle where noted). Atlas: load http://localhost:<port>/maps-site/?v=<fresh-int> and wait for window.ATLAS.ready; handles ATLAS.map, ATLAS.descend(name,poi), ATLAS.chart(name,poi), ATLAS.exitChart(), ATLAS.fly(name), ATLAS.cityReg, ATLAS.cityAnchors. Sim: load http://localhost:<port>/?v=<fresh-int>#s=epeshu and wait for window.ANNALS.ready.
- **P1.** setMaxZoom ease-down (desktop, iphone13). ATLAS.descend('Kanae'), wait for moveend, then ATLAS.map.panBy([1800,0],{animate:false}). Repeat with a drag at z9 from Epēshu into open sea. Log getZoom(), getMaxZoom(), getCenter() and a timestamp on every zoomend/moveend for 3 s. Fail on any zoom change >0.05 without input. Expected today: about 2.3 dz (Kanae) and 1.885 (Epēshu). *(settles: maxzoom-ease-down, refs C3)*
- **P2.** Synthetic-to-melded swap (desktop, iphone13). With cache disabled, load #chart=Sokundo&poi=1. Per rAF, log getZoom(), the count of '.leaflet-pCity-pane img' and ATLAS.cityReg['Sokundo'].url. Record the POI latlng at fly start against worldPOICoord after landing. Take overlay-only shots before and after the swap. Report the POI move in atlas and screen px and the coast rotation in degrees. *(settles: synthetic-to-melded-swap)*
- **P4.** Crossfade residuals in screen px (desktop, iphone13). Centre on Epēshu (1236.1,1415), Summarch at both the printed ring (541.75,49.75; the detector gives 542,50) and the literal (523,58), Sokundo and Damoklir. Shoot each at z7.1, 7.3, 7.5, 7.8 and at the city's own cityMaxZ, once with pCity and pCityWash hidden and once with the tile pane hidden. Pick coast and road edges and report chamfer rms/max in screen px and in atlas px (divide by 2^(z−4)). Static estimate for Epēshu: about 7.7-8.5 atlas px rms (8.48 atlas px chart-to-print ≈ 84 screen px at z7.3). Target after registration: ≤2 screen px at z7.3. *(settles: crossfade-double-image, canon-art-fit-by-box, refs C2, tc-4)*
- **P5.** Hordon/Maeges end state (desktop). Load #chart=Maeges cold. For 6 s log getZoom(), getMaxZoom(), the overlay src, and every setView call via a wrapper, so the ensureArrival retries at 1.7, 3.4 and 5.1 s show up. Expected: a 2.33 dz yank to 8, Hordon's plan appearing, then an unanimated snap to about 10.19. With Hordon pre-registered: a 0.14 ease only. *(settles: hordon-maeges-duplicate)*
- **P6.** exitChart clamp (desktop 1200x800). At fitZoom record getCenter(), then ATLAS.chart('Epēshu') and ATLAS.exitChart(). Repeat with 'Seat'. Log the centre after zoomend. About 1113 atlas px east today; pass ≤1 px. Also exit, then ATLAS.fly('Kanae'), and log whether the fly completes. *(settles: setminmax-zoom-race)*
- **P7.** Mid-fly mask callback (desktop, iphone13). Cold cache, ATLAS.descend('Sokundo') from the fit view. Per rAF log getZoom(), getMaxZoom() and the '.leaflet-pCity-pane img' count. Fail if the count drops to 0 mid-fly. *(settles: midfly-mask-callback)*
- **P8.** ensureArrival hijack (desktop, iphone13). ATLAS.descend('Epēshu'), then inject a synthetic drag at t=300 ms. Log moveend times and zoom at 1.7, 3.4 and 5.1 s. Fail on any jump after the drag. Expected today: a single teleport of about 4.7 levels. *(settles: ensurearrival-hijack)*
- **P9.** #view boot cap (desktop; settles the disputed view-hash-lossy). Load cold #view=1236,1415,9.5, then #view=542,50,10.5, then a generated ruin at 11. Log getMaxZoom() inside the first worldCityUpdate (before handleHash) and after landing, plus the landed zoom and centre against the request. This decides whether every link deeper than 8 lands at 8 (Epēshu included) or Epēshu keeps 9.885. *(settles: view-hash-lossy)*
- **P10.** Generation jank (pixel7, iphone13, at 4x CPU throttle). Scripted pinch onto an ungenerated town with a PerformanceObserver('longtask'). Report the count and duration of tasks >50 ms. Also time the overlay 'load' of overlay-Epeshu.jpg on Fast 3G against getZoom(); that part belongs to A-U11. *(settles: gen-jank-frame-drops, canon-first-load-popin)*
- **P11.** Vector cost, only if a vector form is chosen (iphone13, pixel7 at 4x CPU throttle, desktop). Build a throwaway page with Leaflet 1.9.4 CRS.Simple. Load synthetic city layers at both ladders: N = 300 / 1,000 / 3,000 and 5,000-30,000 features (1.9k-30k vertices), and the measured sizes 700 / 1,000 / 2,100 / 6,279 / 7,758 features at 2.6k / 8.5k / 23k / 29k vertices. Draw each two ways: SVG merged by class (9-30 L.polygon multipolygons, 3-level arrays, fillRule:'nonzero') and L.canvas({pane:'pCity'}), each with smoothFactor 0 and the default. Run a flyIntoCity-like flyTo from z6 to z9.4 and a scripted two-finger pinch. Use PerformanceObserver('longtask') and per-rAF timestamps. Report animation-frame ms and the single end-of-gesture task (zoomend _project plus moveend _update) separately, at z7.8 and at the city's cityMaxZ (z9.885 for Epēshu). Pass if no frame exceeds ~10-15 ms and the end task stays under 50 ms. *(settles: gap-vecbudget C3/C4, G7-3, vlod VL12/VL17)*
- **P12.** Retina base dropout (desktop2x, iphone13). At z>10.5 over a village, a ruin and Summarch, log the base tile layer's visible tile count and opacity during zoom, and screenshot the plan and wash edge. *(settles: retina-base-dropout)*
- **P13.** Seam in both directions after registration (desktop, iphone13). Once a per-city record exists, at z7.3 and z9.885 over Epēshu, measure the overlay's land-over-sea and sea-over-land area against the print water mask in screen px, then divide by 2^(z−4). Static targets at 0.6x+3E: 91 and 75 atlas px². Pass when the chosen clip and sea fill (an L.imageOverlay in pCityWash, zIndex 2) hide both bands. *(settles: canon-hard-edge-seam, tc-4, tc-5, tc-6, G0-12)*
- **P14.** clip-path on a Leaflet ImageOverlay (iphone13/WebKit, pixel7, desktop 1366x768@1). Apply a throwaway percent clip-path: polygon() from the islet-filled print-land ring (try DP0.5 and DP0.25) to the Epēshu pCity <img> through the className option or a class rule. Centre on (1236.1,1415) at z7.3 and z9.885. Screenshot with the tile pane hidden and with the pCity pane hidden. Check that land-over-sea is hidden, that both seam bands are handled, that there is no hairline at the clip edge, that the clip stays crisp at 9.885, and that it tracks the img through a button zoom (CSS transform), a pinch and flyIntoCity / ATLAS.descend('Epēshu') (per-frame _reset and _animateZoom transform writes). Log rAF frame times during the fly with and without the clip. If the clip fails, test an inline SVG data-URI mask-image with -webkit-mask-image as the fallback. *(settles: G8-1, G8-8, G8-9)*
- **P16.** Fill tone (G8-4). At 0.6x+3E, with the clip and a sea-toned L.imageOverlay in pCityWash at zIndex 2, sample screen RGB inside the clipped band 1 and compare with the print sea mean [159,152,117]; target ΔE well under the measured wash 14.1. Repeat after a forced washRefresh to confirm the fill stays above the wash. *(settles: G8-4, G8-5)*
- **P15.** River crossings (desktop). For the 18 generated plans with a printed river within 2.5·FOOT_R (G0-7), shoot at z7.8 and cityMaxZ with pCity shown and hidden, and record whether the plan hides or contradicts the printed river. Repeat after any anchor move. *(settles: G0-7, G0-10)*
- **P17.** Chart-conditioned build time on phones (pixel7 and iphone13, also at 4x CPU throttle). Load prototypes/epeshu-chart.html, call build() with seed 'epeshu' 5 times, and run the build (growRibs, extractBlocks, makeParcels, footprint) under PerformanceObserver('longtask'), recording performance.now() deltas and longtask entries. Node v22 baseline: about 1.2 s (1,177-1,375 ms). Decide whether a port can build off the gesture path (idle callback after moveend, cached) or must ship static output. *(settles: G7-10)*
- **P18.** DPR crispness (desktop2x 1440x900@2, iphone13). ATLAS.descend('Epēshu'). Take device-pixel screenshots of the .leaflet-pCity-pane img at z8.3, 8.885, 9.385 and 9.885, centred on (1236.1,1415). Measure the edge-gradient width in device px across a building outline. Confirm softness begins at z8.3 on DPR 3 and z8.885 on DPR 2. *(settles: G0-18)*
- **P19.** Era check (desktop). Switch setEra among modern, war and imperial with the Epēshu overlay and clip at z7.8. Confirm the overlay, clip and POIs do not move. Record the wash tone mismatch on war and imperial (expected ΔG≈29, ΔB≈21 in Leponnia) as a colour item, not a registration item. *(settles: G9)*
- **R1.** Leaflet rounding at rest (desktop, iphone13). ATLAS.fly('Epēshu'), then map.setZoom(7.37,{animate:false}). Read the getBoundingClientRect corners of the .leaflet-pCity-pane img and compare them with map.latLngToContainerPoint of the cityBox corners (x 1211.87-1260.33, y 1389-1441 via px()). Do the same for a tile img's position against latLngToContainerPoint of its tile corner. Pass at ≤1 screen px. Repeat per rAF during ATLAS.fly from the fit view and log the maximum. *(settles: refs C1, vlod VL10, subpixel-rounding)*
- **R2.** Debug swipe audit (desktop, iphone13). Centre on Epēshu (1236.1,1415) and on Summarch (541.75,49.75). At z7.0, 7.5 and 8.0 run curBase.setOpacity(1) and CITYVIEW.overlay.setOpacity(1), set style.clipPath='inset(0 50% 0 0)' on the overlay img, and take a screenshot. Pick coast and dotted-road edges on both sides of the clip line. Report the offset in screen px, and divide by 2^(z−4) for atlas px. Baseline for Epēshu: 8.48 atlas px rms chart-to-print, about 84 screen px at z7.3. *(settles: refs C13, OQ12)*
- **R3.** H2z camera mapping (gated on street=1, Street 3/4). From atlas z=9.885 centred on (1236.1,1415), compute res = 2^(4−z) atlas px per CSS px, convert to metres through 800/768 and CELL, and set the sim camera height to res·canvasWidth/(2·tan(fov/2)), top-down, at the focus mapped by atlas=(1060,1240)+grid·800/768. Render the sim, overlay it on the atlas crop of the same CSS size, and report the focus-point offset and the coast offset in atlas px. *(settles: refs C12, RA-swisstopo-3d, OQ24)*
- **S13.** Sim fingerprint under a cityLabels move (own-sim S13 is code reading only). Load the sim at http://localhost:<port>/?v=<fresh-int>#s=epeshu, wait for window.ANNALS.ready, run ANNALS.simDays(400) and record JSON.stringify(ANNALS.stats()). Repeat against a throwaway copy of index.html in the mktemp directory, with every Epēshu cityLabels dx/dz in buildEpeshuMapData shifted by +20 m. Pass: identical stats. Also log the flyTo targets for 'Epēshīn Forum', 'Whitestreet', 'the Marble Quarter' and 'Wood Quay' in both builds to show which frozen-view poses (SV1, SV3, SV6, SV8) move. *(settles: own-sim S13, OQ21)*
- **S22.** Atlas gates vs sim s.entries (own-sim S22, never measured). Atlas: load maps-site/?v=<int>, wait for ATLAS.ready, ATLAS.descend('Tamaron') (then Epēshu-window cities such as Lepon the Old), and read the gate bearings that cityApproaches/mkGate produce. Sim: load #s=epeshu, find the same settlement in ANNALS.world.settlements, and convert each s.entries {x,z} to a bearing from the settlement centre (sim −z = north). Report the per-gate angular difference in degrees and any unmatched gates on either side. No pass bar yet; record as baseline. *(settles: own-sim S22)*
- **G0-3.** cityCanvasSha by point convention (G0-3's hash differences are inferred, not recomputed). Desktop profile. For each of Tamaron, Sokundo and Kanae at the HEAD anchors: ATLAS.descend(name), wait for moveend, sha256 ATLAS.cityReg[name].url in the page. Then set ATLAS.cityAnchors[name] in turn to the ring centre (Tamaron ◉ 1659,1554; Sokundo ○ 1478.0,1450.0; Kanae ○ 1523.0,1490.0), ring−(1,0) and ring−(1,1). For each, delete the cityReg entry and its cache, descend again, re-hash, and log the gate and sea sets. Pass: confirms whether the three conventions give three different hashes, and which sea sets appear (expected Tamaron [319.3,1.6] at the ring centre vs [317.7,113.6,3.5] at ring−(1,1)). *(settles: G0-3, OQ5, OQ6)*

---

## 8. Open questions

Deduplicated from the five sections and grouped by theme. These are questions the evidence could not settle; some need the owner or DM, some a browser probe, some a source that was egress-blocked. `findings.json` `open_questions[].raw` names the section entries each one merges.

### Shift mechanics still unmeasured

- **O1.** view-hash-lossy: the two checks disagree on the boot cap. Does maxBounds clamp the boot centre away from every anchor, giving a cap of 8 everywhere, or does Epēshu keep 9.885? Probe P9 decides it.
- **O2.** Hordon/Maeges: is the cold-path end state (snap back to about 10.19 on Hordon's plan) reached within the first ensureArrival retry on real networks, or does the view sit at z8 until 3.4-5.1 s? (P5)
- **O3.** Which share of the observed shift is cityBox placement, setMaxZoom auto-zoom-out, or the nearest-city overlay swap? Not measured (task 19, after Mobile 5; P1, P4).
- **O4.** How large are the Epēshu and Summarch misfits against the PatrinorModern coast, rivers and roads under cityBox, in world px and degrees of rotation, measured in a browser (POLISH 'Anchoring: measure shift budget', task #19; P4, R2)? Pick coast and road features on the atlas and on the overlay and report per-feature offsets. That decides whether a straight fit with a better scale is enough, or whether Helmert, affine or piecewise warping is needed.

### Anchors and point conventions

- **O5.** Sokundo and Kanae targets: the G0-2 detector finds clean ○ rings 5.81 and 7.45 px from their anchors, but the marker-bias dissent's local fit found Kanae off-ring and Sokundo unstable. A crop check is needed before the trio is re-cut.
- **O6.** Summarch ring centre: (542.0,50.0) from the G9 pixel-centre detector and the ◉ marker −(0,2) rule, against (541.75,49.75) from the earlier tie-point fit. The 0.25-px gap is most likely a convention difference, settled by the G0-3 ruling (OQ5).
- **O7.** Was the Summarch anchor (523,58) vs printed glyph (542,52) offset deliberate (shoreline fit) or drift from the 'snap to printed ◉' pass? Same for Penthelon (113 px).
- **O8.** G0-3: the per-convention cityCanvasSha differences are inferred from sampleCityMask's rounding, not recomputed (probe G0-3).
- **O9.** canon C15: the checks say eight and ten ◉ cities have no anchor; the named list implies nine. This needs a recount.
- **O10.** gap-consumers C11: the checks give about 29 against 17 anchors reaching gazetteer.json through the anchor path. The second check's named list of 17 is used.
- **O11.** Were the Shengo and Rhup anchors re-snapped in city-anchors.json after the sim grid coordinates were fixed? If so, the sim gridX/gridZ need regenerating from the anchors.
- **O12.** After the anchors move, how many generated plans gain or lose printed-river crossings, and do Aldrakhar, Iet Ford, Bash Dargis and Stony Ford change class as G0-10 predicts? (P15)

### Canon charts: orientation, scale, control points, bending

- **O13.** Is the DM Epēshu chart north-up? The tie fits reject rotation, but the chart carries no compass (Summarch's chart does). The atlas projects it unrotated into cityBox; the sim's hand-placed labels and landmarks fit no single similarity transform of the chart, and some sim district offsets would match the POIs better under a different rotation.
- **O14.** Do the canon charts need rotation at all? Only translate plus uniform scale was tested (tc-4). A rotation would force either an offline pre-rotated plate (TILES_V bump, A-U11 conflict) or a warp, which was measured and rejected in G0-11.
- **O15.** True and display scale: should the Epēshu chart be ruled to a true scale (the DM never gives it one; the Summarch bar, 1.82 px/m, could suggest a house scale but is not evidence for Epēshu)? Is the DM-drawn city art meant to be at true scale relative to the atlas at all? Should the canon city stay an exaggerated inset (about 10-16x the atlas scale) whose rim is fitted onto the atlas coast, roads and river, or should the zoom-through shift scale continuously? That needs the length of the Cyrikon mile (MI_PER_NATIVE is in Cyrikon miles).
- **O16.** Control points: which chart pixel should sit on the atlas ring-dot (cityBox centres the whole DM image on the anchor, not on a charted landmark such as the Forum or the harbour mouth; the owner/DM must name them)? Which atlas features can serve as GCPs inside or next to each canon footprint: coast inflections in the z5 mask, the harbour mouth, river lines and the Summarch river crossings, dotted-road exits or road entries in traced-roads.json, the ring-dot? Is the ring-dot the city centre or a symbol, and can the DM say what it marks? 'Traced road network (USER FLAG)' is still unchecked in POLISH.md, and traced-roads.json has no points within 60 px of Epēshu.
- **O17.** Is the DM chart's straight western sea edge an artistic simplification, so the chart must be fitted to the atlas coast, or does it encode a harbour shape the atlas lacks? The atlas is authoritative either way, but the fit's tolerance depends on the answer.
- **O18.** How much may canon DM art be bent to fit the atlas? Helmert/straight keep the drawing exactly; polynomial1 shears it; TPS/polynomial2+ rubber-sheet it (rubber-sheeting seems unwelcome). This needs an owner ruling (OQ10), since registration bends art to the atlas and the atlas is never moved.
- **O19.** Does the Epēshu painting's depicted extent (walls, quays, coast) match the FOOT_R capital box of about 48x52 atlas px, or should control points on coast, river and dotted-road gates set the box? This needs the in-browser shift measurement.
- **O20.** Is a wall drawn on the Epēshu chart? Three files disagree; the wall-ring strain figures for an Epēshu warp (G0-14) rest on a wall traced by eye while sibling files say no separable wall is drawn, so they stay on hold.
- **O21.** Are overlay-Epeshu.jpg and the z3 charts/epeshu pyramid the same art? q08 reports a mean RGB difference of 13.6 and left it unsettled.
- **O22.** Does the live wiki's Epēshu page (it has a 'Map' heading in the snapshot) or the Map-of-the-Wide-World page state a scale or embed the Epēshu chart with a bar? The wiki host was unreachable through the egress proxy, so contentIndex.json and the Leaflet config were not read.
- **O23.** Does the DM hold higher-resolution masters or vector sources (MFCG seed, permalink or GeoJSON export; Inkarnate, Wonderdraft or Illustrator originals) for the Epēshu and Summarch charts? The 3914x4200 and 4245x3951 pyramids are the best on disk and do not look vectorised from a line source; their original size (before the 0.73 overlay downscale) is unknown. Were the charts made in Watabou's MFCG or another generator? A GeoJSON export would replace all tracing (OQ17).
- **O24.** If the canon overlay is ever pre-warped: should the chart pyramids (charts/epeshu, charts/summarch) used by enterChart stay unwarped as the DM's original (probably yes, since chart mode is its own modal CRS, but POIs must then use two different transforms)? Output format: JPG with parchment-filled corners, or WebP/PNG with alpha? It interacts with washMake's seam and the mobile byte budget.
- **O25.** Should cityMaxZ, which repeats the footR/aspect maths instead of calling cityBox, be folded into the single override function, or keep its own copy reading the same record?
- **O26.** Should one registration record per city (control points from chart px to atlas px) drive the meshed-world overlay, the chart POIs and the modal chart pyramids together, or should modal chart mode (enterChart, its own chart-px frame) stay modal as the Region-chart item allows ('keep modal where the geometry doesn't fit')? Ownership spans filigree slice C, the living H2z poster and the Mobile atlas lane (OQ11).
- **O27.** Which source governs Epēshu's interior: the DM-drawn city chart (overlay-Epeshu / charts/epeshu / chart-pois) or the sim's seeded plan? 'Atlas geography is authoritative' does not settle it, because the PatrinorModern print has no interior. Today the two disagree by 103-489 m per district.

### Seam, delivery and the filigree route

- **O28.** Does the evenodd fill-rule of clip-path polygon() work on iOS Safari 15? MDN BCD has no entry for it. The single islet-filled ring avoids the question but loses islets (P14).
- **O29.** A-U11's swap mechanism is unspecified in docs/mobile/units.json: setUrl (a bounds override is then independent of the swap) or a second registerCityOverlay call (tears down wash, stubs and POIs)? If it re-runs registerCityOverlay, the canon clip, the fill and the override must survive overlayCache.delete and the prov reset.
- **O30.** Should registration of the canon charts ship default-on as a base-atlas data fix (declared UG8 regions, a rebase after Mobile 9), or behind filigree=1 as part of slice C? R18 only governs the table map.
- **O31.** B-38 clips city contours to the chart's own coast, which treats the canon chart as truth, while the stated constraint makes the print authoritative. Should the owner rule that registration supersedes B-38's chart-coast clip? Changing B-38 means re-integrating the bible (Path B) before Filigree 2.
- **O32.** Slice letters: the bible's prerequisites table says F3-C is the fiction stack and F3-D the city; the Filigree 2 script and POLISH say C is the city and D the stack. Which should a registration prerequisite cite?
- **O33.** The README §7 route into Filigree 2: own-atlas v2's holding check names three limits, and only the first (live read at run time) is visible in the check text; limits (2) and (3) are unknown. Does anything gate or hash a README §7 edit (any workflow preflight that sha-checks docs/filigree/README.md)? None was found among the items, but it was not verified directly.
- **O34.** When Filigree 2 runs relative to Mobile 9 determines whether registration costs a Filigree 2 rebase; filigree-2-plan.js never re-reads state.json, so nothing checks this automatically.

### Rivers, roads and scale constants

- **O35.** Who supplies plan-usable rivers? 18 generated plans plus Epēshu sit near a printed river, and no item owns the consumer. It should queue after Mobile 9/14 with an atlas_fn_sha rebase.
- **O36.** Which mile constant governs: the printed bar (659 atlas px per 600 mi = 0.910 mi/px) or MI_PER_NATIVE=600/723 (0.830)? 723 is undocumented; the printed bar is measurable on the tiles. The traced Sun Road favours 0.83 (568 vs 560 leagues) only if a league is exactly 3 mi (OQ16).
- **O37.** What is a league? Mountain Wall implies 3.0 mi (900 km = 186 leagues); Lepon implies 2.5 mi (20 leagues = 50 mi). Is a Cyrikoi mile equal to an English mile at all, and what is a River-Measure (about 1.11 mi on the printed bar)?
- **O38.** Should the sim's epeshu roads follow the atlas's traced roads (atlas authoritative), at the cost of changing the seeded world and its determinism fingerprint?
- **O39.** Who owns a shared traced road graph used by both the atlas city sheet (Filigree C) and the sim street view (street track)? Is its source of truth the art-px trace or the atlas-px warp?

### Vector city: tracing, generation, cost, ownership

- **O40.** Can a street layer for the canon charts come from the negative space of the ground class (Epēshu 5,089 polygons / 105,656 vertices at DP 1) or from a centreline graph via thinning (AutoTrace -centerline or a skeletonize step on the thin white or grey road strokes)? Both are unmeasured (G0-20), as is how cleanly badge and legend in-filling restores the building fabric beneath.
- **O41.** Why do V8 (17,323 Epēshu blobs ≥12 px on overlay-Epeshu.jpg) and G4/G0-20 (23,184 on the charts/epeshu/3 pyramid) differ? The source-resolution explanation is an inference; the check text is truncated.
- **O42.** Could the canon city charts be traced to footprints (blocks, quays, walls) to give hover hit geometry while the DM art stays the painted surface? accurate-mapping.md's U-Net plus watershed pipeline is unproven on a single hand-drawn chart; interactive tracing in a tool step may be the realistic path.
- **O43.** genCityCanvas exposes no geometry. Should a vector form of generated plans re-implement the plan in a sibling function, or refactor the pinned function under an owner-approved atlas_fn_sha/cityCanvasSha rebaseline? If slice C draws the procedural towns as vectors, cityCanvasSha (a hash of the invented data: canvases) becomes null and fails UG3 in every later capture-gated run: should the vector layer sit beside the raster registration (keeping the data: URL) or replace it, with the baselines re-taken after Mobile 9/14?
- **O44.** Pre-existing: cityCanvasSha differs on the 1x desktop (likely canvas text hinting). Any generator-touching vector work inherits that per-class comparison.
- **O45.** Does the prototype's zero-area-building defect have causes beyond the own-stamp repair path? About 87 remain after clearing the stamp.
- **O46.** Per-frame cost of a Canvas-renderer city of N footprints (N = 300 / 1,000 / 3,000) during a flyIntoCity descent and a pinch on the iphone13/pixel7 profiles: nothing in web-performance.md or docs/mobile measures it, and SwiftShader fps is ungated (P11). Where does the Canvas-renderer cutoff sit for about 17k footprints against an offline-rasterised atlas-frame pyramid? That comparison was noted as needing tools/mobile-capture.js after Mobile 5-9; it was not measured here and is outside this phase's probes.
- **O47.** Should vectors appear only after moveend (raster stays the in-flight surface) or be redrawn per 'zoom' frame by a custom layer? Leaflet's Renderer CSS-scales during animation (VL9). This belongs to the living-chart track (H2z) and needs its decision.
- **O48.** Should the atlas adopt preferCanvas:true globally, as web-performance.md recommends, or a pCity-only L.canvas renderer? Global adoption touches every pane while the mobile atlas lane (Mobile 5-9) is mid-flight.
- **O49.** Does a vector city fit filigree slice C (painted city, pooled edges) and R4/R7? Vectors may need to be invisible hit geometry plus hover halos rather than a new visible style; that is filigree's call. Does a vector Epēshu traced from the canon chart count as 'drawn from the canon chart' (B-33) and accepted print under R5, or does the owner need to rule on tracing and warping canon art?
- **O50.** Who owns an atlas vector city and a shared city generator? Filigree slice C owns genCityCanvas, registerCityOverlay, washMake and sampleCityMask (by the README §7 reuse map) under mobile sha pins; living may not touch them (LC12); street may not write maps-site (ST1), leaving the link to 'Sim ↔ atlas continuity'. Is it a slice-C amendment (Filigree 2 re-run) or a new track or item, and does it get a pane despite the UG7 / Filigree 2 pane freeze?
- **O51.** Is the 'one geometry for atlas and sim' goal wanted at all, given that the fabric's scale cannot be shared (only the trace and the transform can)? Should a single Epēshu plan file (streets, quays, gates, the Blue Temple footprint) be the shared source both apps read, so the atlas vector city and the sim's 3D streets share vertices? If so, which track writes it?

### Sim, living and cross-app identity

- **O52.** Already open: LC7 — is a page change into the sim acceptable as the 'transitive layer', or must the zoom stay in the atlas? First exhibit (default: the Epēshīn Forum)? This ask partly answers it and should be put back to the owner with an in-atlas 2D vector city named as an option (OQ20).
- **O53.** Does H2z's crossfade source become the vector city instead of the raster overlay, and should the poster or a sim top-down view be shot from a pose derived from the atlas frame (R3), so the hand-off is geometrically aligned rather than a name-keyed cut?
- **O54.** Already open: LC17 — the sim's dragon in #s=epeshu: rule (a), (b) or (c). It matters here only as the precedent and queue for any #s=epeshu world-gen change.
- **O55.** Already open: LC4 — which creatures move. Not affected by this ask.
- **O56.** If the DM chart governs, may the sim's cityLabels and extraLandmarks be re-registered to it? This is fingerprint-safe by code reading but moves Street SV1/SV6 (and SV3/SV8), so it is cheapest before Street 1 freezes views. May the sim's Epēshu street plan be rebuilt on chart geometry? That is a world-gen change with an LC17-style re-baseline, after Street 4 (OQ21).
- **O57.** Unmeasured sim facts: the simDays(400) fingerprint invariance under a cityLabels move (own-sim S13) is code reading (probe S13); agreement between atlas gates (cityApproaches/mkGate) and the sim's s.entries (own-sim S22) has never been measured (probe S22).
- **O58.** One canonical district and landmark name list (article and spelling: 'Marble Quarter' vs 'the Marble Quarter', 'Amphitheatre' vs 'Amphitheater', 'The Fairy Arch' vs 'Fairy Arch'), so goto and chart links and a shared vector city can match by name. Should 'Sim ↔ atlas continuity' grow from name vocabulary to district-level targets (goto=<district>, cityLabels resolution) and a shared coordinate contract, given that LC7's default exhibit 'Epēshīn Forum' is ignored by the sim's goto today (OQ22)?

### References and tools that could not be verified here

- **O59.** How does swisstopo georeference its historical Dufour/Siegfried sheets (per-sheet control points, transform type, rubber-sheeting, the LV03 to LV95 shift)? The swisstopo georeferencing pages and Heitzler et al. (e-Perimetron 13(2)) are egress-blocked, so upstream registration is still an inference and VL3 rests on search summaries.
- **O60.** Does the Siegfried figure (a 0.7 mm / 35 m average deviation over ten points, 1888 guidelines) actually appear in Heitzler et al., e-Perimetron 13(2)? So far it comes only from a search summary.
- **O61.** Does ch.swisstopo.zeitreihen use the generic OpenLayersWMTSLayer component, and does the viewer crossfade between sheets (unread in the geoadmin code)? Is DEFAULT_FEATURE_IDENTIFICATION_TOLERANCE (10 px) passed to the identify backend, and is the light base map (ch.swisstopo.leichte-basiskarte_world.vt) mounted as a VECTOR layer?
- **O62.** Were the era tile sets (tiles-war/, tiles-imperial/) themselves registered to the modern atlas, or only cut on the same grid? (G9 shows their ink matches modern within 1 atlas px at every anchor.)
- **O63.** Owner references still unidentified or unverified: is github.com/jeantimex/tokyo the owner's Tokyo post? What is Bay Atlas's real stack (MapLibre + PMTiles + Mapzen terrain + OSM is unverified; no repo was found)? The Lombard Street exhibit and traffic demo are still unidentified; the owner would need to supply the links.
- **O64.** Game wiki maps (Fextralife, MapGenie and similar Leaflet CRS.Simple sites): no fetchable primary source showed how they register city or underground sub-maps into the world frame. Only Leaflet's CRS.Simple tutorial was verified (VL13).
- **O65.** Tooling unknowns, moot unless a build step or plugin is ever allowed: no-SRS gdalwarp with atlas world px (y down) as target — what sign does the output geotransform's y axis get, and is negating y needed (not run)? Could Allmaps' renderer work on CRS.Simple with an identity or custom proj4 definition (Viewport and WarpedMapList hard-default to Web Mercator; not tried)? Is there a primary source for leave-one-out validation in a georeferencing tool (gdal.org, docs.qgis.org, allmaps.org and pro.arcgis.com were egress-blocked; only a search snippet)? Is Leaflet.ImageOverlay.Rotated's nested-div technique correct under Leaflet 1.9.4 zoomSnap 0 fractional flyTo and under pinch zoom (needs a browser test, not allowed in this job)?

### Gaps in the check record

- **O66.** Truncated dissents: the failing-check objections for G0-2, G0-5, G0-10, G0-13, G0-15, G8-1, G8-2, G8-3, G8-7, G8-11, G9-2, G9-8, G7-6 and G7-9 are cut off in the only record, as are those for canon C02 and C21, reg R8, gap-mobile C4, and the disputed R6, C06, tc-2, tc-8, G7-13 and others marked 'truncated' in the sections, so their exact objections are not known. G0-13's objection appears to concern the 7-10 POI displacement figures, and G8-7's may concern whether a runtime mask may read the cached MASKQ[key].m without editing the pinned function.
